# Re:Zero — Japanese light novel Volume 16 prospective deep reading

This source-facing freeze belongs to the synchronized V16 transaction, not an already passed publication receipt. Current high-water advances only after the complete gate. The32 protected predecessors retain exact bytes/defaults/local grades; V17 and every later narrative witness remain unopened. All authored work is inside the requested Re-Zero root.

## 0. Governing thesis and prospective boundary

V16 makes the year's growth real before demonstrating that real growth is not an all-purpose defense. Subaru can train, learn manners, acknowledge somebody else's expertise and be loved without having ceased to intervene badly. Otto's officer work is real and underappreciated, not a provisional joke that can disappear into friendship; yet his larger commercial ambition remains his own. Emilia can lack ordinary knowledge, fail at cooking and still recognize a concealed plan or face a powerful official without being maneuvered by racial insult. Beatrice can want company and act for Emilia while also misunderstanding directions, clothes and a supposedly haunted inn. Garfiel can love books and family and still respond to overwhelming strength with a reflexive attack, retreat and the fear that anything short of strongest is useless. None of these people becomes intelligible through one scalar of maturity.

The important comparison is not ordinary life versus irrelevant filler. Cooking provides an actual domain in which Wilhelm needs Reinhard, whereas a title and sword reputation have made honest ordinary approach almost impossible. A shared meal exposes different forms of deference: Subaru asks Emilia and Beatrice to taste their own failure; Julius consumes Anastasia's burned offering before politely requesting a shorter cooking time; Felt accepts Reinhard's extraordinary skill without accepting every presentation he arranges for her. The meal supports genuine pleasure and tentative repair without abolishing resentment, political risk or a relative's ability to wound. Wilhelm actually asks for advice and Reinhard actually answers as a person, but the interrupted movement is not a completed apology or reconciliation.

Institutional power is equally plural. Subaru's accepted knight role now has peer/public recognition and a retrospectively attested capital medal, but that does not specify every national enrollment. Otto's office is explicit without settling his old oil claim. Heinkel holds a formal deputy post, and Priscilla attributes the family headship to him despite his failures in the roles people value; Reinhard's supreme strength cannot simply replace that reported legal and observed emotional structure. Julius, Ferris and Reinhard can reject an order outside the deputy's present jurisdiction while still experiencing the pressure of rank. Priscilla's intelligence and ability to recognize another person's proud refusal coexist with her admitted amusement at damaging a family scene. Her acceptance of Liliana's vocational independence is genuine counterevidence to indiscriminate possession, not acquittal for what she did at breakfast.

Care also has an information problem. Subaru can withhold Julius's private family account for a reason different from withholding RBD, while Emilia rightly refuses to accept a pleasant outing as the whole explanation of his tactical purpose. Wanting her honesty to survive need not mean keeping her ignorant. Conversely, the missing origin conversation cannot be supplied by the intervening year: the record does not narrate its contents or identify its recipients. A timeskip is neither a receipt nor an exhaustive diary of nonoccurrence.

The final square scene turns the apparent language of connection into an attack on judgment itself. Courtesy, gratitude, shared pleasure and applause become compatible with a bleeding child's visibly resisted coercion. The child never agrees merely because the crowd feels united. Subaru's own applause is depicted inside the contamination, not as the analyst's endorsement of his free moral judgment. The return leaves more than a remembered fatal body: he recognizes that he did not recognize evil while participating in it. That horror is the volume's sharpest complication of the idea that accumulated experience or a generous heart must always protect the self.

The source boundary is exact. V15's292 claims,60 questions and10 forecasts were fixed before V16; the latter horizon remains SHA256279e4a504f39376b8b7083a95841ef28ee10416976c93bc9d69bb20b9bf1b413. The separate Arc04 checkpoint and seven model/review pairs completed their own gates before this reading; the immediate source/final base is c3dd17c4e6fd44f1e7759b347c00603e3e39186b. Thirty-two protected freezes/checkpoints/model-review artifacts stay byte-identical. Their reviewed defaults and local documentary grades do not silently become V16 grades. Ram's chronology remains OPEN and the Garfiel/Frederica attribution correction remains operative. V17+, supplements, IF, WN, adaptation and the new import report have not been opened.

## 3. Event identity, body and reached ending

The entering state is living RZ-E-018, continuing the V12 arrival through V15's rescue/liberation and ceremony. The one-year interval, obstacle-course falls, a blue-stone explosion, seasickness, ordinary sleep, startled reactions and comic talk of being killed do not add a return. The source explicitly calls the final restoration the first death-loop in a year. The square event is actual Subaru death18; the observed park arrival supplies RZ-E-019, the nineteenth arrived analytical segment. These analytical state identifiers do not prove that discarded worlds are destroyed or independently survive.

At the new point Liliana is again proposing that Subaru buy refreshments while the women hear her song. His first trip's later shopping, Rachins encounter and square atrocity belong to discarded continuation, while the earlier camp meetings, first failed negotiation, family confrontation and shared year's ordinary history are before that point. Counterparts do not inherit the lost square's applause, injury or testimony. Subaru's remembered original first meetings with Otto/Rachins were already nonmutual before this reset; a new return does not heal the older asymmetry.

The narrator says the point is only十数分 before the death and explicitly says Subaru has not yet recognized that timing. It would be an information error to give the stunned E019 Subaru the narrator's complete clock model. Emilia and Beatrice can see pallor, crouching and nausea and ask what happened. Neither receives his death-trigger mechanism or the discarded scene from those visible symptoms. The bodily restoration and retained horror are different changes.

The living E019 endpoint supplies no successful counterattack, safe disclosure, recovered crystal, awake Puck, delivered Gluttony letter, rescued child, completed family reconciliation or decoded Wrath mechanism. Such outcomes are future questions, not meanings hidden inside《了》. Emotional sharing and the lethal cascade are observed; exact activation, range, immunity, causation and countermeasure remain unresolved.

## 4. Source-facing findings and exact evidence routes

### V16-B01 — A year of practice is not another life

**Exact source:** p-001#B0001–0073.

Subaru's obstacle course, learned breakfall and Clind's teaching/design place genuine bodily work inside a year since Sanctuary/Beatrice's contract. Garfiel and children like the 'secret facility'; Otto's different taste is also real.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Repeated failed attempts, a timeskip and exaggerated danger are not actual deaths or proof of combat parity. Clind's broad usefulness and others' protective reservations do not independently prove an offense.

### V16-B02 — A cooled towel has several agents

**Exact source:** p-001#B0017–0021/B0064–0072; p-003#B0010–0015/B0057–0072.

Petra asks for a towel/cooling, Emilia supplies cooling because ice would take time, Beatrice brings it and credits Petra, and Subaru thanks her. Beatrice's wanted memories/notebook and valued maternal hairstyle coexist with tangles and grooming labor.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Do not assign the cooling to Beatrice, invent a named spell or literal camera, or make unwanted hair-pulling comfortable because Subaru wants her to make friends.

### V16-B03 — Petra's own memory is disclosed now

**Exact source:** p-003#B0001–0040; p-005#B0278.

The new household uses the larger former main residence, not a rebuilt annex. Petra tells Subaru her earlier appearance-based impression and takes pleasure in his actual acknowledgment; she is explicitly thirteen later in the volume.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Her received disclosure now is not Ram's or another loop's childhood memory. Growth and competence do not make adult romance or prove a previously promised outing happened.

### V16-B04 — Mimi, Beatrice and unfamiliar company

**Exact source:** p-003#B0044–0088/B0160–0180.

Mimi's actual visit/reunion contrasts with Beatrice's wary hiding and concern for her hair. Outsiders notice automatic lap/hand habits; familiar care has a public TPO dimension.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Mimi's 'daughter' framing is not parentage. The people not accompanying this visit are not located everywhere in the city/world, and encouragement toward friendship is not blanket bodily consent.

### V16-B05 — Guest voice and acquired office

**Exact source:** p-003#B0091–0180.

Ram distinguishes guest politeness from Balus banter; her Mimi search is also an escape pretext. Joshua self-identifies as envoy before Emilia tells Subaru he is Julius's brother. Subaru performs learned etiquette and is introduced as Emilia's actual knight; Otto is explicitly chief internal-affairs officer.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Reader frontmatter names are not earlier Subaru knowledge. Hyperbolic Otto-death/stomach talk adds no event; appointment does not specify wage, repayment or every national enrollment.

### V16-B06 — A corrected misconception is not negotiated romance

**Exact source:** p-003#B0180–0195; p-005#B0271–0287.

Emilia now knows a kiss does not cause pregnancy while further knowledge remains limited. Later narration explicitly reports no romantic progress despite affectionate words, Subaru's night care and his strong feelings.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Correction cannot retroactively supply the V13 kiss's informed assent, a complete curriculum, sex, pregnancy, a new kiss or an agreed relationship.

### V16-B07 — The invitation takes initiative through Puck

**Exact source:** p-005#B0001–0140.

The invitation/contact about a high-purity catalyst draws Emilia's immediate interest in dormant Puck before full camp consultation. Subaru and Otto interpret host leverage and choose the trip, while Rabbit credit remains limited by lack of a body/proof.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** A lure is not acquisition or a fulfilled contract. A future no-sighting report and public Whale/Sloth standing do not prove Rabbit extinction or official equivalent credit.

### V16-B08 — The boys have different roles and limits

**Exact source:** p-005#B0140–0180/B0219–0238.

Otto resents decisions made before consultation and carries administrative work without abandoning his merchant dream. Garfiel is fifteen, chooses milk and is weak with alcohol; he addresses two older companions as different brothers.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Intended stress-relief teasing may backfire by Otto's own account. Lifelong-office jokes are not employment contracts, present drinking rules do not prove prior party intoxication, and a shield role is not total obedience.

### V16-B09 — Daily letters and partial ordinary freedom

**Exact source:** p-005#B0181–0202.

Garfiel eagerly writes frequent/daily rough-script letters to Ryuzu; Otto reports Frederica also writes and expects fewer letters, not a verified frequency comparison. Ryuzu is at Arlam teaching24 released mana-bodied girls daily life, postponing cohabitation. Subaru supports personhood and privately suspects guilt over discarded copy sacrifice.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Garfiel does not receive the loop guilt when Subaru says nothing. Generic Ryuzu/24 bodies do not identify every old persona, establish completed citizenship or prove that Garfiel explicitly revised his former doll category.

### V16-B10 — A physical vocabulary can misread social attention

**Exact source:** p-005#B0204–0238/B0243–0257.

Garfiel reads Mimi's attention as an invitation to fight; Subaru questions it. The boys respect Otto despite teasing. Otto's Shatranj strength contrasts with Garfiel's repeated losses and Subaru's Othello-type strength; Garfiel's early-sleep height belief is specific.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** No shown Shatranj win follows. Frederica 'stealing growth' is a joke, not biology. Mimi's attention and Garfiel's attachment to Ram do not establish mutual romance; his strength-oriented social reading is not a general-purpose threat detector.

### V16-B11 — Nightly care receives no answer

**Exact source:** p-005#B0258–0288.

Subaru knocks, changes flower water daily and talks nightly to the still breathing, sleeping Rem. Recalled Emilia/Beatrice support helps him continue; the narrator explicitly says more than a year has passed without her reply.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** The supportive lines are remembered, not both women present at this night visit. Imagined awake Rem respect is Subaru's projection, not her current consent, memory or recovered agency.

### V16-B12 — Patronage and chosen education

**Exact source:** p-006#B0003–0043.

Roswaal works western political support and accepts the trip. Annerose is aboutten and governs the Milord house with Clind's help, with a strong Emilia preference; Petra chooses learning and Frederica promises protection, so Subaru does not veto her opportunity.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Useful patronage does not erase Roswaal's objective or debts. Protective concern about Clind is not a witnessed offense, and Petra is neither a passive possession nor an invulnerable adult.

### V16-B13 — A freer manner is not a solved bond

**Exact source:** p-006#B0030–0077.

Ram pushes more openly with Roswaal while her response to姉様 explicitly lacks recovered sister history. Beatrice accepts escort responsibility after hiding/listening and protesting Roswaal's teasing.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Subaru's 'mutual understanding' impression is not a reciprocal romantic confession or proof past dependence ended. Banter does not broadcast private soul/redo disclosures or supply limitless mana.

### V16-B14 — Travel competence has an exact domain

**Exact source:** p-006#B0079–0117/B0169–0177.

Otto plans a long safe route without camping, using Fulfhu/Patrasche; the actual journey reaches twelve days. Subaru can drive four familiar dragons, not any animal, while tickling causes an actual wobble and complaint.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** No crash or fatality occurs. Garfiel's permitted running and others' narrow travel experience do not establish their inexhaustibility; about-ten-days en route and twelve-day arrival are not a reset.

### V16-B15 — A private political lesson and an actual apology

**Exact source:** p-006#B0118–0168.

Otto briefs Emilia on election timing and rival camps while Subaru is outside that exchange. Emilia asks questions, receives reasons to share responsibilities and actually apologizes to Otto for the initiative problem.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Reported Felt/Priscilla/Crusch developments are not direct visits or a complete universal power ranking. Subaru's later question shows he did not automatically hear this lesson; this apology is not his old overnight apology.

### V16-B16 — A city of rules and incompletely known origins

**Exact source:** p-006#B0178–0244/B0280–0325.

Entry entails a written city-specific legal pledge. Canals, gates and the inn provide material infrastructure; Beatrice explains old records of a lake-built trap but cannot name its intended target.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Recorded knowledge is not a prior lived visit. No magical enforcement, all-purpose trap target or independently verified summoned founder follows from urban design.

### V16-B17 — The untranslated roar

**Exact source:** p-006#B0245–0285.

A water dragon appears; Beatrice supplies attributed bonding lore. Subaru suggests using Otto to insult it, but Patrasche's roar drives the encounter before that translation is performed.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** Do not turn the proposal into executed cruelty or exact speech, or infer a new blessing from an outcome whose meaning Otto/Subaru do not understand.

### V16-B18 — Familiar culture and inferred Hoshin

**Exact source:** p-006#B0300–0390; p-013#B0152–0169.

The inn's Wafu form and later kimono/cooking traditions move Subaru differently from his companions. He strongly infers Hoshin was a compatriot summoned predecessor and plans further inquiry.

**Boundary/counterevidence:** His確信/おそらく reasoning is not independent confirmation of origin, summoner, purpose or no-return law. Hearing foreign culinary terms does not give others his Earth biography.

### V16-B19 — A peer knight and an attributed attraction

**Exact source:** p-006#B0391–0498/B0530–0546.

Julius acknowledges Subaru's changed knight standing and meets him as a peer; Ana/Julius recall a capital award meeting. Julius describes誘精の加護, Beatrice's attraction is visible, and concern about Joshua permits correction of rudeness rather than collective family hostility.

## 5. Subaru, Emilia and Beatrice: competence without a single maturity scale

Subaru's training is not imaginary merely because it cannot answer Sirius. The opening gives his learned breakfall, a designed obstacle course and sustained practice; the later stretching contest makes this ordinary bodily work visible again. His continued tendency to compare himself with Julius does not turn every deficit into total incapacity. Equally, a real knight title does not make an uninformed intervention good. Otto was already negotiating toward acceptable terms when Subaru's prospective help and Liliana's retelling worsened the scene. The relevant failure is not a lack of benevolence but proceeding without the colleague's actual information, timing and consent. Accepting exclusion from the next appointment-making visit is a small received correction, not a universal cure. [V16-B01/B24–25/B37/B48]

Emilia's new knowledge is equally differentiated. Knowing that a kiss does not produce pregnancy neither supplies the missing informed terms of the older kiss nor proves a complete sexual curriculum. A year of affection has explicitly not produced romantic progress. Her limited cooking knowledge is real: she has to taste the failed food rather than be praised for intent. So is her active learning from Otto, including a received explanation of rival camps and the practical reason a candidate's unannounced arrival can burden a host. In the breakfast confrontation she does not need hidden magical pressure or secretly feigned innocence to ask the right question. Her refusal to follow Heinkel's racial bait is an exercise of the same other-regarding anger that can coexist with ordinary naivety. The narrator expressly cancels the thought that she had been pretending all along. [V16-B06/B15/B25/B32/B40/B43]

The later walk adds an important limit to Subaru's version of protecting that virtue. Emilia sees that seeking Liliana is more than recreation and questions the use of a friend as leverage. Subaru can reasonably distinguish knowledge of political calculation from adoption of its worst practices; he cannot infer her assent simply because he has explained his own reason. She says she will consider it. This received exchange differs from his silence about Julius's private family report and from his much older inability to disclose RBD. Privacy, manipulation, pedagogical explanation and supernatural enforcement are not a single secret-keeping motive. [V16-B49]

Beatrice is not reducible to either a dependent child or an endlessly capable ancient expert. She wants plural shared memories while fearing new company and unwanted hair-pulling. Her confident directions fail; her meal fails in a different way from Emilia's; a mundane drunken Otto corrects her frightened ghost interpretation. She can nevertheless speak for Emilia against an insult without waiting for Subaru's order, then accept Emilia's thanks and cease escalating. These are actual new relational choices rather than proof that every anxiety vanished. Her refusal to heal a stomped foot is explicitly anger, so it cannot calibrate the limit of her magic. Daily accumulated mana, broken-Gate maintenance and the sleep-time health rationale for room sharing must be retained together; neither 'permanently zero' nor 'restored old reserve' fits the new observations. [V16-B02/B04/B22/B33/B36/B40/B51]

The E019 ending selects a new acute state for Subaru and only a visible new concern for Emilia/Beatrice. He remembers having applauded an atrocity while unable to recognize it; they see his pallor, crouching and distress without having witnessed the square. A scene continuation must not make either woman explain the discarded child killing or have Subaru calmly announce a fully understood checkpoint before he has recognized the timing. [V16-B57]

## 6. Otto, Garfiel and the household: help has independent subjects

Otto's office is no longer merely an implication of the work everyone leaves him. The narrator names him 筆頭内政官. His repeated merchant ambition remains a real competing preference, not an appointment-refusing catchphrase the analysis may discard. His resentment at being consulted after a decision has been taken has a procedural point even when he would have recommended the same trip. Emilia's actual apology and his later teaching distinguish receiving a complaint from having a colleague's interests automatically solved. No salary, oil compensation, waiver or final crystal bargain is enacted by calling him indispensable. The hints of a concealed extra purpose do not identify salvage, name the V15 youth or license a later-volume answer. [V16-B05/B07–08/B15/B25/B36–37/B39]

Garfiel's respect for Otto is expressly stated despite the joking hierarchy. His letters, rough handwriting, games, milk, earlier alcohol mistake and early-sleep theory give ordinary texture to somebody whose combat speech can otherwise dominate reconstruction. Mimi's attention is hers, while Garfiel reads her possible social approach as a fight request; neither that response nor Subaru's interpretation establishes Garfiel's reciprocal affection. Nor does the actual beast-arm first strike against Reinhard become defensive merely because Garfiel has experienced captivity and fear before. Reinhard has not attacked him. The effortless stop and involuntary retreat from an offered hand make a new failure condition legible: being strong but not strongest can feel useless to Garfiel. His request to leave is received, but Subaru's expectation that a night will suffice is not the outcome. Garfiel and Mimi remain absent at their last report; no offpage recovery, romantic consummation or mother encounter is supplied. [V16-B08–10/B27–28/B31/B39]

The kinship material must also keep its subjects. Ryuzu's teaching of24 released mana-bodied girls is a concrete partial implementation of life beyond surveillance, not all-person identity resolution or complete citizenship. Garfiel receives no explicit account of Subaru's discarded sacrifice guilt because Subaru withholds it. Frederica's own letters and protection of Petra are her acts, not generic extensions of Garfiel's body/power. Petra chooses further education and is explicitly thirteen; protective reservations about Clind do not establish an enacted offense, and an educational choice does not make her an adult. Ram's more assertive manner toward Roswaal is visible while her sister's autobiographical history remains missing. A new degree of ease does not adjudicate the old nearly-seven-year/nine-year contradiction, restore a horn or prove reciprocal romance. [V16-B03/B09/B11–13]

Roswaal can be absent doing useful western patronal work while remaining the author of a destructive private condition. Subaru's intensified fear of others' deaths is explicitly connected to that condition in the garden conversation. There is no new test showing that the visible sanction prevents action, and no demonstrated abandonment of the teacher-restoration objective. The Ram15/Garfiel15/Roswaal15 historical models remain scoped records; their local documentary reviews are not silently repeated at this new horizon. [V16-B12–13/B34]

## 7. Wilhelm and the Astrea family: a first step is not forgiveness

Wilhelm's night conversation matters because it is not only an elder giving excellent counsel. His wish to die holding a sword frightens Subaru, whose new concern about others' deaths is intelligible without Wilhelm knowing its supernatural cause. Wilhelm can name self-effacement as both a virtue and weakness, admit wanting more opportunity to speak of his wife, accept bodily help and disclose shame about his grandson. He says that he has three great regrets, not that this scene enumerates all three. The grandfather's confessed grave words and avoidance behind revenge make the need for approach concrete while leaving the full cause and the grandson's internal response unavailable. [V16-B34–35]

Subaru's advice has a proper boundary: apologize without owning the right to be forgiven. His relaying Reinhard's actual wish to hear the Whale story supplies an available opening, not a transparent view into Reinhard's heart. The following morning turns that opening into a small performed request. Wilhelm has trouble with an ordinary cooking task; Reinhard is demonstrably good at it; Wilhelm actually asks for advice and receives a personal smile. Subaru's imagined years of future repair are moving precisely because they remain prospective. The meal disperses explicitly without reconciliation. [V16-B35/B40–43/B46]

Heinkel's interruption gives both legal and personal constraints. He is the Guard deputy, and Priscilla explains that he holds Astrea family headship; his lack of the sword-name is stated by him. The three knights can refuse his present order because their special duty removes this jurisdiction, not because rank never matters. Felt's base can be threatened through estate/headship without Reinhard's strength automatically transferring the property. The actual accusations about Theresia inflict harm, but neither family silence nor the claim of a dead mother's curse proves that Reinhard caused her death. Briefly perceiving Heinkel's own pain does not acquit the cruelty. [V16-B43/B45/B47]

Reinhard's ordinary friendship and vulnerability revise a strength-only model. He affirms Subaru as a friend despite the remembered earlier dismissal; Garfiel recoils from his offered hand, and Reinhard briefly looks saddened and withdraws it; he cooks, praises Felt and is visibly affected by family words. Felt's rough speech is not simple incapacity for manners or blind dependence. Her order that he hold himself proudly is a protective act inside her chosen political relation. It momentarily changes his demeanor without curing the history. Neither Subaru's private intention to repay him nor this warm reunion is an enacted apology for Subaru's particular old wrong. [V16-B28/B31–33/B38/B40/B45]

Julius's explanation further resists a tidy family verdict. Former Guard command/search/resignation and the recorded recommendation of Theresia are not the same evidence class as unconfirmed involvement in a royal abduction. The kingdom's concern about containing a possible defector is not proof of present disloyalty. Julius acknowledges incomplete motives and his own impropriety in speaking of another household; only Subaru receives the report. A character model may use Julius's worry as evidence about Julius without converting every proposition he relates into omniscient history. [V16-B47–49]

## 8. Peers, candidates and artists: contrasts that do not cancel

Julius's courtesy at breakfast is not proof that he lacks personal anger. His later thanks to Subaru and acceptance of criticism reveal the cost of restraining that anger under a knightly role. Subaru is not required to accept proxy credit for an outburst he thinks poorly delivered; he actually apologizes for misdirecting new anger toward Julius. Their discussion of the Yuri alias, actual stretching and competitive music banter changes the immediate mood without erasing remembered hurt. The narrator preserves residue. Julius's professed 嗜み does not demonstrate a virtuoso concert, and a friend's advice to remove the heart's armor is not a completed withdrawal from public office. [V16-B19/B40–41/B46–48]

Crusch's missing autobiographical memory must not become ethical vacancy. She has her own reason to pursue Gluttony and insists that Subaru receive a share of the information for Rem. Her gratitude, candidate support and cooking are actual present choices. Her weaker control of wind-reading is a limit, not complete loss of self or an infallible remaining lie detector. Ferris's medical anger at Subaru has a named cause: the destroyed Gate and dependence on Beatrice's mana handling. Ferris's continued care, provocative teasing checked by Wilhelm, dress and successful cooking provide additional domains, not evidence for an inferred sexual orientation, settled reciprocal romance or unlimited revival. Neither receives RBD history merely because they have treated Subaru before. [V16-B29–32/B40/B43]

Anastasia's host care and commercial leverage can be sincere together. She offers a contact, not an already owned crystal; the Gluttony intelligence is still being collected, and Joshua's departure to fetch the letter is not delivery of its contents. The warm kimono reveal and badly burned food contrast with bargaining competence without proving all-domain incompetence. Kiritaka's jealous stone-throwing is actual harmful conduct, while the civic emergency-broadcast initiative attributed to him is a useful contribution. Either fact remains material when the other is true. [V16-B20/B24/B30/B39–42]

Priscilla's new contrasts require revision, not rehabilitation by one good act. She admits bringing Heinkel to change the spectacle for amusement, knows more about rivals than an obliviousness model allows, and begins a lethal stroke stopped by Al. Al's successful plea and restraint disprove 'no agency' despite his apparent low standing; Schult's carrying out the unconscious man is also actual work. Later Priscilla accepts Liliana's proud vocational refusal and apologizes for discourtesy. That acceptance is real counterevidence to 'she tolerates no refusal'; it does not undo her earlier sabotage. Her world-as-garden aesthetic is a self-model, not literal universal ownership or decoded supernatural providence. [V16-B44–45/B50]

Liliana's case likewise separates artistic competence from social judgment. The songs genuinely affect listeners, but the novel supplies no general rule that every audience response is an Authority. Her negotiation advocacy badly misstates Subaru's terms; her park mediation later recognizes tension; her refusal grounds dignity in an inherited itinerant vocation. Subaru explicitly describes his affection for Beatrice as nonromantic, and his own rap must not be credited to Liliana. The main LN's retrospective acquaintance is admitted evidence at V16; it does not authorize opening an external side story. [V16-B22–24/B42/B50–51]

## 9. Pristella and Sirius: connection without consent

Pristella's written entry pledge, canal traffic, water-control explanation, crafts, food and emergency-listening routine make civic order material before an attacker exploits a public square. The city law is not shown to enforce itself magically. Beatrice's account of old trap-building comes from records and expressly lacks a target; Subaru's Hoshin inference comes from familiar cultural forms and lacks a summoner or mechanism. These are different kinds of knowledge, not proofs of an omniscient city plan. A clocktower in the square is not automatically city hall or one of the four cardinal control towers. [V16-B16–18/B21/B38/B40/B42/B52]

Sirius gives a name and office before Subaru understands her effect. His proposed contract alert is interrupted; Rachins's possible signal to Reinhard is not sent. Fear and threat become a seemingly reasonable shared good feeling, and Subaru abandons his earlier perceived need to call Beatrice. The focalized reassuring language should therefore be read as part of the danger, not adopted as the analyst's judgment. Courtesy and thanks do not make the encounter safe. No precise trigger, range or immunity has been isolated merely because voice, applause, attention and feeling occur together. [V16-B53–54]

The decisive counterevidence to the crowd's happy interpretation is Lusbel himself. He is bleeding, chained, resisting and asking to be saved. Sirius presents a flattering volunteering/family story while the crowd applauds; the child has not thereby freely chosen danger. His actual resistance has a different evidential status from her account of Musran, Ina and Tina. The fatal fall and matching lethal cascade strongly indicate a harmful connection, but not a decoded complete Authority or exact casualty census. Subaru dies in the cascade; he does not deliberately kill himself and Reinhard does not arrive to complete the considered rescue. [V16-B55–56]

The return's retained horror is accordingly not merely fear of repeating a pain. Subaru knows he participated without being able to recognize what was wrong. That is a new limit on moral self-trust and experienced competence, not proof that all prior growth was false or that he freely chose a stable preference for cruelty. Ordinary affection, crowd praise and a benevolent purpose must now be evaluated alongside the possibility of imposed judgment. At EOF the reader knows a short interval is closing; Subaru has not yet identified it. What he can warn, whom he can reach and what action would protect rather than repeat the child's fate are genuinely unanswered. [V16-B57]

Address is recipient- and scene-selected. Anastasia's regional contractions, Garfiel's clipped boastful forms, Julius's formal self-restraint, Ferris's teasing self-reference, Ram's guest/household switch and Sirius's excessive courtesy do different work. Their exact source lines remain in §4; an English 'quirk' is not a replacement voice model. The unused year is not evidence of every new lexical habit or a blanket narration-to-character disclosure.

## 12. Stable current claim register

The current register contains340 formulations:292 individually reviewed predecessors and48 new claims. Scene-local current/new language preserved from an earlier formulation belongs to its named source/event, not the latest default. E019 is the reached V16 endpoint. Preserving a prior statement does not claim a new test or broad source-scope extension. The unenumerated year is not proof that an unshown promise either happened or never happened. Preserved afterword/current/this-volume wording is local to its cited prior source: for example C292 retains V15's Arc04 afterword, while C336 records V16's Arc5 opening. Neither is a new claim that the V16 afterword closes Arc04.

## 13. Every predecessor adjudicated

All292 entering formulations were actually read and individually reviewed against the complete V16 source:187 PRESERVE,74 STRENGTHEN,23 REVISE,8 OPEN; no DOWNGRADE or REJECT is needed. These counts describe the resulting dispositions, not a numerical claim of fidelity. Every exact earlier formulation is recoverable below; its full earlier evidence ancestry also remains in V15 §12 and the exact preserved master snapshot. §12 gives each successor's full current wording. The repeated PRESERVE convention means that this exact earlier proposition was individually checked, no material contrary test/fulfilled obligation/new recipient was established, and its earlier evidence ancestry remains intact at V15 §12; it is not claimed newly corroborated or extended. Preserved source-local 'current/new' terms refer to their historical scene, not E019 or a new test. Absence from the unenumerated year is not proof either of fulfillment or never occurring.
